# Scenarios

Generated by `cargo run -p lngap-harness --bin scenarios` against a throwaway regtest
chain (txids are from that run and change every run). Heights are absolute regtest
heights; the channel is funded at height ~204 in every scenario.

The PS* sections run the PoS-venue graph (pos-factchain branch, plan step 7): the
contract output is funded directly and the graph played by hand — the force-close
commitment (~240 vB, unchanged by the venue swap) is excluded from their
transaction tables, and their balances are the payout outputs' on-chain totals.

## T1 — Full game cooperative; final update folds V to user; cooperative close

**Expected:** funding tx + 1 close tx only; user 110k, hub 90k

**Final balances (Alice's/user's channel; on-chain if closed, else off-chain):** user 109500 sat, hub 89500 sat

| 204 | `funding` | harness | `d74f85a3d942d86f41ac7e219b9571bbd9c152222caa68fe980e9ade82ef0d4b` |
| 205 | `coop_close` | user | `b23b07354ab3c6d17a74848ad4dabbd1c4339b0593d6619b5c97094c642b32d8` |
